targetSales: 5200000 },\n  { month: '9月', sales: 4820000, customers: 1460, avgSpend: 3301, targetSales: 4800000 },\n  { month: '10月', sales: 5150000, customers: 1530, avgSpend: 3366, targetSales: },\n];\n\nconst FILTER_OPTIONS: FilterOption[] = [\n  { label: '全期間 (1-12月)', value: 'all' },\n  { label: '上期 (1-6月)', value: 'first-half' },\n  { label: '下期 (7-12月)', value: 'second-half' },\n  { label: '直近3ヶ月', value: 'last-3' },\n];\n\nconst App: FC = () => {\n  const [period, setPeriod] = useState<PeriodFilterType>('all');\n  const [simParams, setSimParams] = useState<SimulationParams>({\n    customerRate: 0,\n    spendRate: 0,\n  });\n\n  const periodFilteredData = useMemo(() => {\n    switch (period) {\n      case 'first-half':\n        return BASE_MONTHLY_DATA.slice(0, 6);\n      case 'second-half':\n        return BASE_MONTHLY_DATA.slice(6, 12);\n      case 'last-3':\n        return BASE_MONTHLY_DATA.slice(-3);\n      case 'all':\n      default:\n        return BASE_MONTHLY_DATA;\n    }\n  }, [period]);\n\n  const simulatedData = useMemo(() => {\n    const custMultiplier = 1 + simParams.customerRate / 100;\n    const spendMultiplier = 1 + simParams.spendRate / 100;\n\n    return periodFilteredData.map((d) => {\n      const customers = Math.round(d.customers * custMultiplier);\n      const avgSpend = Math.round(d.avgSpend * spendMultiplier);\n      const sales = Math.round(customers * avgSpend);\n      return {\n        ...d,\n        customers,\n        avgSpend,\n        sales,\n      };\n    });\n  }, [periodFilteredData, simParams]);\n\n  const kpiCards = useMemo<SummaryCardData[]>(() => {\n    const totalSales = simulatedData.reduce((acc, cur) => acc + cur.sales, 0);\n    const totalCustomers = simulatedData.reduce((acc, cur) => acc + cur.customers, 0);\n    const avgSpend = totalCustomers > 0 ? Math.round(totalSales / totalCustomers) : 0;\n    const totalTarget = simulatedData.reduce((acc, cur) => acc + cur.targetSales, 0);\n\n    const salesAchievementRate = totalTarget > 0 ? ((totalSales - totalTarget) / totalTarget) * 100 : 0;\n\n    let custChangeRate = 0;\n    let spendChangeRate = 0;\n    if (simulatedData.length >= 2) {\n      const latest = simulatedData[simulatedData.length - 1];\n      const prev = simulatedData[simulatedData.length - 2];\n      custChangeRate = ((latest.customers - prev.customers) / prev.customers) * 100;\n      spendChangeRate = ((latest.avgSpend - prev.avgSpend) / prev.avgSpend) * 100;\n    }\n\n    return [\n      {\n        title: '期間合計売上高',\n        value: `¥${(totalSales / 10000).toLocaleString(undefined, { maximumFractionDigits: 1 })}`,\n        unit: '万円',\n        subValue: `目標: ¥${(totalTarget / 10000).toLocaleString(undefined, { maximumFractionDigits: 1 })}万円 (達成率 ${Math.round((totalSales / totalTarget) * 100)}%)`,\n        changeRate: salesAchievementRate,\n        isPositive: salesAchievementRate >= 0,\n        iconName: 'sales',\n      },\n      {\n        title: '期間総来客数',\n        value: totalCustomers.toLocaleString(),\n        unit: '人',\n        subValue: `月平均: ${Math.round(totalCustomers / simulatedData.length).toLocaleString()}人 / 直近推移`,\n        changeRate: custChangeRate,\n        isPositive: custChangeRate >= 0,\n        iconName: 'customers',\n      },\n      {\n        title: '期間平均客単価',\n        value: `¥${avgSpend.toLocaleString()}`,\n        unit: '円/人',\n        subValue: '1顧客あたりの平均利用額',\n        changeRate: spendChangeRate,\n        isPositive: spendChangeRate >= 0,\n        iconName: 'avgSpend',\n      },\n    ];\n  }, [simulatedData]);\n\n  const handleCustChange = (e: React.ChangeEvent<HTMLInputElement>) => {\n    setSimParams((prev) => ({ ...prev, customerRate: Number(e.target.value) }));\n  };\n\n  const handleSpendChange = (e: React.ChangeEvent<HTMLInputElement>) => {\n    setSimParams((prev) => ({ ...prev, spendRate: Number(e.target.value) }));\n  };\n\n  return (\n    <div className=\"dashboard-container\">\n      <header className=\"dashboard-header\">\n        <div className=\"header-title-group\">\n          <h1 className=\"header-title\">店舗・事業KPIダッシュボード</h1>\n          <p className=\"header-subtitle\">\n            売上・客数・平均単価の月次推移およびシミュレーション分析\n          </p>\n        </div>\n\n        <div className=\"filter-bar\">\n          {FILTER_OPTIONS.map((opt) => {\n            const isActive = period === opt.value;\n            const btnClass = isActive ? 'filter-btn filter-btn-active' : 'filter-btn';\n            return (\n              <button\n                key={opt.value}\n                className={btnClass}\n                onClick={() => setPeriod(opt.value)}\n              >\n                {opt.label}\n              </button>\n            );\n          })}\n        </div>\n      </header>\n\n      <section className=\"sim-panel\">\n        <div className=\"sim-title\">\n          <SlidersHorizontal size={16} />\n          <span>売上影響シミュレーション（客数・客単価の増減感度）</span>\n        </div>\n        <div className=\"sim-controls\">\n          <div className=\"sim-slider-group\">\n            <label className=\"sim-label\" htmlFor=\"cust-slider\">客数変動率:</label>\n            <input\n              id=\"cust-slider\"\n              type=\"range\"\n              min=\"-30\"\n              max=\"30\"\n              step=\"1\"\n              value={simParams.customerRate}\n              onChange={handleCustChange}\n              className=\"sim-slider\"\n            />\n            <span className=\"sim-val-display\">\n              {simParams.customerRate > 0 ? Math.round((d.sales / d.targetSales) * 100) : 0,\n    }));\n  }, [data]);\n\n  const sortedRows = useMemo(() => {\n    return [...rows].sort((a, b) => {\n      let comparison = 0;\n      if (sortColumn === 'month') {\n        const aMonth = parseInt(a.month, 10) || 0;\n        const bMonth = parseInt(b.month, 10) || 0;\n        comparison = aMonth - bMonth;\n      } else {\n        comparison = a[sortColumn] - b[sortColumn];\n      }\n      return sortDirection === 'asc' ? comparison : -comparison;\n    });\n  }, [rows, sortColumn, sortDirection]);\n\n  const handleSort = (column: SortColumn) => {\n    if (sortColumn === column) {\n      setSortDirection((prev) => (prev === 'asc' ? 'desc' : 'asc'));\n    } else {\n      setSortColumn(column);\n      setSortDirection('desc');\n    }\n  };\n\n  const renderSortIcon = (column: SortColumn) => {\n    if (sortColumn !== column) {\n      return <ArrowUpDown size={14} style={{ opacity: 0.5, marginLeft: 4 }} />;\n    }\n    return sortDirection === 'asc' ? (\n      <ArrowUp size={14} style={{ color: 'var(--accent-primary)', marginLeft: 4 }} />\n    ) : (\n      <ArrowDown size={14} style={{ color: 'var(--accent-primary)', marginLeft: 4 }} />\n    );\n  };\n\n  return (\n    <div className=\"table-card\">\n      <div>\n        <h2 className=\"chart-title\">月次実績一覧</h2>\n        <p className=\"chart-subtitle\">各月の売上、目標、来店客数、客単価および達成率の一覧詳細</p>\n               <span>目標売上 (円)</span>\n                  {renderSortIcon('targetSales')}\n                </button>\n              </th>\n              <th className=\"th-cell\">\n 0 : val));\n    setSimParams((prev) => ({ ...prev, customerRate: clamped }));\n  };\n\n  const handleSpendChange = (val: number) => {\n    const clamped = Math.max(-20, Math.min(20, isNaN(val) ? 0 : val));\n    setSimParams((prev) => ({ ...prev, spendRate: clamped }));\n  };\n\n  const handleResetSimulation = () => {\n    setSimParams({\n      customerRate: 0,\n      spendRate: 0,\n    });\n  };\n\n  return (\n    <div className=\"dashboard-container\">\n      <header className=\"dashboard-header\">\n        <div className=\"header-title-group\">\n          <h1 className=\"header-title\">店舗・事業KPIダッシュボード</h1>\n          <p className=\"header-subtitle\">\n            売上・客数・平均単価の月次推移およびシミュレーション分析\n          </p>\n        </div>\n\n        <div className=\"filter-bar\">\n          {FILTER_OPTIONS.map((opt) => {\n            const isActive = period === opt.value;\n            const btnClass = isActive ? className=\"sim-panel\">\n        <div className=\"sim-title\">\n          <div className=\"sim-title-label\">\n            <SlidersHorizontal size={16} />\n       <div className=\"sim-slider-group\">\n            <label className=\"sim-label\" htmlFor=\"cust-slider\">客数変動率 (-30%〜+30%):</label>\n            <input\n              id=\"cust-slider\"\n
